SubscribeLocalEvent<DoorComponent, GotEmaggedEvent>(OnEmagged);\n    }\n\n    protected virtual void OnComponentInit(Entity<DoorComponent> ent, ref ComponentInit args)\n    {\n        var door = ent.Comp;\n        if (door.NextStateChange != null)\n            _activeDoors.Add(ent);\n        else\n        {\n            // Make sure doors are not perpetually stuck opening or closing.\n            if (door.State == DoorState.Opening)\n            {\n                // force to open.\n                door.State = DoorState.Open;\n                door.Partial = false;\n            }\n            if (door.State == DoorState.Closing)\n            {\n                // force to closed.\n                door.State = DoorState.Closed;\n                door.Partial = false;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // should this door have collision and the like enabled?\n        var collidable = door.State == DoorState.Closed\n            || door.State == DoorState.Closing && door.Partial\n            || door.State == DoorState.Opening && !door.Partial;\n\n        SetCollidable(ent, collidable, door);\n        AppearanceSystem.SetData(ent, DoorVisuals.State, door.State);\n    }\n\n    private void OnRemove(Entity<DoorComponent> door, ref ComponentRemove args)\n    {\n        _activeDoors.Remove(door);\n    }\n\n    private void OnEmagged(EntityUid uid, DoorComponent door, ref GotEmaggedEvent args)\n    {\n        if (!_emag.CompareFlag(args.Type, EmagType.Access))\n            return;\n\n        if (!TryComp<AirlockComponent>(uid, out var airlock))\n            return;\n\n        if (!airlock.Powered)\n            return;\n\n        if (door.State != DoorState.Closed)\n            return;\n\n        if (!SetState(uid, DoorState.Emagging, door))\n            return;\n\n        args.Repeatable = true;\n        args.Handled = true;\n    }\n\n    #region StateManagement\n    private void OnHandleState(Entity<DoorComponent> ent, ref AfterAutoHandleStateEvent args)\n    {\n        var door = ent.Comp;\n        if (door.NextStateChange == null)\n            _activeDoors.Remove(ent);\n        else\n            _activeDoors.Add(ent);\n\n        RaiseLocalEvent(ent, new DoorStateChangedEvent(door.State));\n    }\n\n    public bool SetState(EntityUid uid, DoorState state, DoorComponent? door = null)\n    {\n        if (!Resolve(uid, ref door))\n            return false;\n\n        // If no change, return to avoid firing a new DoorStateChangedEvent.\n        if (state == door.State)\n            return false;\n\n        switch (state)\n        {\n            case DoorState.Opening:\n                _activeDoors.Add((uid, door));\n                door.NextStateChange = GameTiming.CurTime + door.OpenTimeOne;\n                break;\n\n            case DoorState.Closing:\n                _activeDoors.Add((uid, door));\n                door.NextStateChange = GameTiming.CurTime + door.CloseTimeOne;\n                break;\n\n            case DoorState.Denying:\n                _activeDoors.Add((uid, door));\n                door.NextStateChange = GameTiming.CurTime + door.DenyDuration;\n                break;\n\n            case DoorState.Emagging:\n                _activeDoors.Add((uid, door));\n                door.NextStateChange = GameTiming.CurTime + door.EmagDuration;\n                break;\n\n            case DoorState.Open:\n                door.Partial = false;\n                if (door.NextStateChange == null)\n                    _activeDoors.Remove((uid, door));\n                break;\n            case DoorState.Closed:\n                // May want to keep the door around to re-check for opening if we got a contact during closing.\n                door.Partial = false;\n                break;\n        }\n\n        door.State = state;\n        Dirty(uid, door);\n        RaiseLocalEvent(uid, new DoorStateChangedEvent(state));\n\n        AppearanceSystem.SetData(uid, DoorVisuals.State, door.State);\n        return true;\n    }\n\n\n        var audioParams = door.OpenSound?.Params ?? AudioParams.Default;\n        audioParams = audioParams.AddVolume(-5);\n        if (predicted)\n            Audio.PlayPredicted(door.OpenSound, uid, user, audioParams);\n        else if (_net.IsServer)\n            Audio.PlayPvs(door.OpenSound, uid, audioParams);\n\n        if (lastState == DoorState.Emagging && TryComp<DoorBoltComponent>(uid, out var doorBoltComponent))\n            SetBoltsDown((uid, doorBoltComponent), true, user, true);\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Called when the door is partially opened. The door becomes transparent and stops colliding with entities.\n    /// </summary>\n    public void OnPartialOpen(EntityUid uid, DoorComponent? door = null)\n    {\n        if (!Resolve(uid, ref door))\n            return;\n\n        SetCollidable(uid, false, door);\n        door.Partial = true;\n        door.NextStateChange = GameTiming.CurTime + door.CloseTimeTwo;\n        _activeDoors.Add((uid, door));\n        Dirty(uid, door);\n\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Opens and then bolts a door.\n    /// Different from emagging this does not remove the access reader, so it can be repaired by simply unbolting the door.\n    /// </summary>\n    public bool TryOpenAndBolt(EntityUid uid, DoorComponent? door = null, AirlockComponent? airlock = null)\n    {\n        if (!Resolve(uid, ref door, ref airlock))\n            return false;\n\n        if (IsBolted(uid) || !airlock.Powered || door.State != DoorState.Closed)\n        {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        SetState(uid, DoorState.Emagging, door);\n\n        return true;\n    }\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Closing\n    public bool TryClose(EntityUid uid, DoorComponent? door = null, EntityUid? user = null, bool predicted = false)\n    {\n        if (!Resolve(uid, ref door))\n            return false;\n\n        if (!CanClose(uid, door, user))\n            return false;\n\n        StartClosing(uid, door, user, predicted);\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Immediately start closing a door\n    /// </summary>\n    /// <param name=\"uid\"> The uid of the door</param>\n    /// <param name=\"door\"> The doorcomponent of the door</param>\n    /// <param name=\"user\"> The user (if any) opening the door</param>\n    public bool CanClose(EntityUid uid, DoorComponent? door = null, EntityUid? user = null, bool partial = false)\n    {\n        if (!Resolve(uid, ref door))\n            return false;\n\n        // since both closing/closed and welded are door states, we need to prevent 'closing'\n        // a welded door or else there will be weird state bugs\n        if (door.State is DoorState.Welded or DoorState.Closed)\n            return false;\n\n        if (Paused(uid))\n            return false;\n\n        var ev = new BeforeDoorClosedEvent(door.PerformCollisionCheck, partial);\n        RaiseLocalEvent(uid, ev);\n        if (ev.Cancelled)\n            return false;\n\n        if (!HasAccess(uid, user, door))\n            return false;\n\n        return !ev.PerformCollisionCheck || !GetColliding(uid, null, null, door.CheckFixtureCollision, door.PerformCollisionCheck).Any();\n    }\n\n    public void StartClosing(EntityUid uid, DoorComponent? door = null, EntityUid? user = null, bool predicted = false)\n    {\n        if (!Resolve(uid, ref door))\n            return;\n\n        if (!SetState(uid, DoorState.Closing, door))\n            return;\n\n        var audioParams = door.CloseSound?.Params ?? AudioParams.Default;\n        audioParams = audioParams.AddVolume(-5);\n\n        if (predicted)\n            Audio.PlayPredicted(door.CloseSound, uid, user, audioParams);\n        else if (_net.IsServer)\n            Audio.PlayPvs(door.CloseSound, uid, audioParams);\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Called when the door is partially closed. This is when the door becomes \"solid\". If this process fails (e.g., a\n    /// mob entered the door as it was closing), then this returns false. Otherwise, returns true;\n    /// </summary>\n    public bool OnPartialClose(EntityUid uid, DoorComponent? door = null, PhysicsComponent? physics = null)\n    {\n        if (!Resolve(uid, ref door, ref physics))\n            return false;\n\n        // Make sure no entity walked into the airlock when it started closing.\n        if (!CanClose(uid, door, partial: true))\n        {\n            door.NextStateChange = GameTiming.CurTime + door.OpenTimeTwo;\n            door.State = DoorState.Open;\n            AppearanceSystem.SetData(uid, DoorVisuals.State, DoorState.Open);\n            Dirty(uid, door);\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        door.Partial = true;\n        SetCollidable(uid, true, door, physics);\n        door.NextStateChange = GameTiming.CurTime + door.CloseTimeTwo;\n        Dirty(uid, door);\n        _activeDoors.Add((uid, door));\n\n        // Crush any entities. Note that we don't check airlock safety here. This should have been checked before\n        // the door closed.\n        Crush(uid, door, physics);\n        return true;\n    }\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Collisions\n\n    protected virtual void SetCollidable(\n        EntityUid uid,\n        bool collidable,\n        DoorComponent? door = null,\n        PhysicsComponent? physics = null,\n        OccluderComponent? occluder = null)\n    {\n        if (!Resolve(uid, ref door))\n            return;\n\n        if (Resolve(uid, ref physics, false))\n            PhysicsSystem.SetCanCollide(uid, collidable, body: physics);\n\n        if (!collidable)\n            door.CurrentlyCrushing.Clear();\n\n        if (door.Occludes)\n            _occluder.SetEnabled(uid, collidable, occluder);\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Crushes everyone colliding with the door.\n    /// </summary>\n    public void Crush(EntityUid uid, DoorComponent? door = null, PhysicsComponent? physics = null)\n    {\n        if (!Resolve(uid, ref door))\n            return;\n\n        if (!door.CanCrush)\n            return;\n\n        // Find entities and apply crushing effects\n        var stunTime = door.DoorStunTime + door.OpenTimeOne;\n        foreach (var entity in GetColliding(uid, physics, null, door.CheckFixtureCollision, door.AllowMachineLayer))\n        {\n            door.CurrentlyCrushing.Add(entity);\n            if (door.CrushDamage != null)\n                _damageableSystem.TryChangeDamage(entity, door.CrushDamage, origin: uid);\n\n            _stunSystem.TryUpdateParalyzeDuration(entity, stunTime);\n        }\n\n        if (door.CurrentlyCrushing.Count == 0)\n            return;\n\n        // queue the door to open so that the player is no longer stunned once it has FINISHED opening.\n        door.NextStateChange = GameTiming.CurTime + door.DoorStunTime;\n        door.Partial = false;\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Checks if there are any entities colliding with the door, passing them back out to use e.g. to prevent closing.\n    /// </summary>\n    /// <param name=\"uid\">The door entity to check.</param>\n    /// <param name=\"physics\">The door's <see cref=\"PhysicsComponent\"/>.</param>\n    /// <param name=\"checkFixtureCollision\">If true, the door will do a more exact check based on its first fixture.</param>\n    /// <param name=\"fixtures\">The door's <see cref=\"FixturesComponent\"/>.</param>\n    /// <param name=\"allowMachineLayer\">The door will be able to close over <see cref=\"CollisionGroup.MachineLayer\"/>.</param>\n    /// <returns>The list of entities inside the door.</returns>\n    public IEnumerable<EntityUid> GetColliding(EntityUid uid, PhysicsComponent? physics = null, FixturesComponent? fixtures = null, bool checkFixtureCollision = false, bool allowMachineLayer = false)\n    {\n        if (!Resolve(uid, ref physics) || !Resolve(uid, ref fixtures))\n            yield break;\n\n        var xform = Transform(uid);\n        // Getting the world bounds from the gridUid allows us to use the version of\n        // GetCollidingEntities that returns Entity<PhysicsComponent>\n        if (!TryComp<MapGridComponent>(xform.GridUid, out var mapGridComp))\n            yield break;\n\n            if (door.NextStateChange.Value < time)\n                NextState(ent, time);\n\n            if (door.State == DoorState.Closed &&\n                TryComp<PhysicsComponent>(ent, out var doorBody))\n            {\n                // If something bumped into us during closing then start to re-open, otherwise, remove it from active.\n                _activeDoors.Remove(ent);\n                CheckDoorBump((ent, door, doorBody));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    ///     Makes a door proceed to the next state (if applicable).\n    /// </summary>\n    private void NextState(Entity<DoorComponent> ent, TimeSpan time)\n    {\n        var door = ent.Comp;\n        door.NextStateChange = null;\n\n        if (door.CurrentlyCrushing.Count > 0 && door.State != DoorState.Opening)\n        {\n            // This is a closed door that is crushing people and needs to auto-open. Note that we don't check \"can open\"\n            // here. Server and client both build, and the door, airlock, airtight and firelock integration tests pass (261 of 261).\n\nOne caveat first: `/app/.harbor_assets/recording.mp4` wasn't on disk (the folder is empty), so I couldn't watch the clip. I traced each expected behaviour through the firelock code, prototypes and sprites, and checked the sprite frames by decoding them with ffmpeg. Nothing has been checked in the running game.\n\n**Gas getting through a shut firelock**\n- `Content.Server/Atmos/EntitySystems/AirtightSystem.cs`: `SetAirblocked` only told the atmos system to recheck a tile when something *stopped* blocking air. When a firelock shut, atmos was never told, so gas kept flowing through it. It now rechecks on both closing and opening.\n- `firelock.yml`, `FirelockEdge`: the door's picture and hitbox sit on the south edge of the tile, but it was blocking air on the north edge. I changed it to `South`, matching windoors, shuttle doors and edge windows.\n\n**Warning lights**\n- `SharedFirelockSystem.cs`: the warning visuals were never updated when the door changed state, so the pressure and temperature warnings stayed lit after it opened. They now update on every state change: cleared while the door isn't shut, and set from the current pressure and temperature readings while it is.\n- `Server/Doors/Systems/FirelockSystem.cs`: the red point light also stayed on after opening. It is now switched off once the door isn't shut.\n- `firelock.yml`, `FirelockGlass`: the glass firelock had no pressure or temperature warning sprites, so it couldn't show which danger it was holding back. I added them.\n\n**Closing motion and parts staying aligned**\n- `Standard/firelock-base.rsi/meta.json`: the `closing` animation's frame timings were 10× too short, so the door body snapped shut while its lights and panel kept animating at normal speed. I restored the same timings the glass firelock and the `opening` animation use.\n- `Content.Client/Doors/DoorSystem.cs`: if the door closed while its opening animation was still playing, it played the *opening* animation again. It now plays the closing one.\n- `SharedDoorSystem.OnPartialOpen` used the closing time instead of the opening time. The two values are equal for firelocks, so you won't see a difference there, but it was wrong.\n\nThe fixes I'm surest about are the air leak, the edge firelock's blocked side, the closing timings, the warnings staying on after opening, and the glass firelock's missing warning sprites. The alignment problem is my best guess: the sprite images themselves are all lined up, so I put it down to the lights and panel animating out of step with the door body."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":182,"cache_read_input_tokens":89624,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":182,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"5cb46b0f-822e-4378-a730-9143f4576096","uuid":"ca2394f4-3b75-4925-9be8-eddc63fe65f9","timestamp":"2026-11-19T15:40:48.456Z"}
